UCharacter.UnicodeBlock [java.lang.Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) |---[java.lang.Character.Subset](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/character_subset-0000001054518456) |---|---ohos.global.icu.lang.UCharacter.UnicodeBlock
    
    public static final class UCharacter.UnicodeBlock
    extends [Character.Subset](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/character_subset-0000001054518456)  
  
---  
**[icu enhancement]** ICU's replacement for [Character.UnicodeBlock](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/character_unicodeblock-0000001054678444). Methods, fields, and other functionality specific to ICU are labeled '**[icu]** '. A family of character subsets representing the character blocks in the Unicode specification, generated from Unicode Data file Blocks.txt. Character blocks generally define characters used for a specific script or purpose. A character is contained by at most one Unicode block. **[icu] Note:** All fields named XXX_ID are specific to ICU.Field Summary Modifier and Type | Field | Description

[h2]PRIVATE_USE public static final [UCharacter.UnicodeBlock](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ucharacter_unicodeblock-0000001054440141) PRIVATE_USE Same as public static final int PRIVATE_USE_AREA. Until Unicode 3.1.1; the corresponding block name was "Private Use"; and multiple code point ranges had this block. Unicode 3.2 renames the block for the BMP PUA to "Private Use Area" and adds separate blocks for the supplementary PUAs.  [h2]PRIVATE_USE_AREA public static final [UCharacter.UnicodeBlock](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ucharacter_unicodeblock-0000001054440141) PRIVATE_USE_AREA Same as public static final int PRIVATE_USE. Method Detail [h2]getInstance public static [UCharacter.UnicodeBlock](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ucharacter_unicodeblock-0000001054440141) getInstance​(int id) **[icu]** Returns the only instance of the UnicodeBlock with the argument ID. If no such ID exists, a INVALID_CODE UnicodeBlock will be returned. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
id  |  UnicodeBlock ID  
**Returns:** the only instance of the UnicodeBlock with the argument ID if it exists, otherwise a INVALID_CODE UnicodeBlock will be returned. [h2]of public static [UCharacter.UnicodeBlock](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ucharacter_unicodeblock-0000001054440141) of​(int ch) Returns the Unicode allocation block that contains the code point, or null if the code point is not a member of a defined block. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
ch  |  code point to be tested  
**Returns:** the Unicode allocation block that contains the code point [h2]forName public static [UCharacter.UnicodeBlock](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ucharacter_unicodeblock-0000001054440141) forName​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) blockName) Alternative to the [Character.UnicodeBlock.forName(String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/character_unicodeblock-0000001054678444#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678444__forName-java_lang_String-) method. Returns the Unicode block with the given name. **[icu] Note:** Unlike [Character.UnicodeBlock.forName(String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/character_unicodeblock-0000001054678444#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678444__forName-java_lang_String-), this only matches against the official UCD name and the Java block name (ignoring case). **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
blockName  |  the name of the block to match  
**Returns:** the UnicodeBlock with that name **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  if the blockName could not be matched  
[h2]getID public int getID​() {icu} Returns the type ID of this Unicode block **Returns:** integer type ID of this Unicode block
